| Can't even remember why Rogue and I were always at each other's throats. All I remember is the good times – and how few of them there were. Now I've got one night to make it up to her. World NodeRef definitions/streaming owners, full actor and animation closure, numeric TweakDB names, spoken subtitle/VO payload joins and installed/save behavior have not been validated by this structural pass. Raw direct references, actor bindings, choices, event locstrings and community entries remain in the evidence shards.

## Required acceptance cases before reuse

Exercise normal completion and each named alternate outcome; interrupt and return at every scene handoff; start with spatial/item/actor predicates already satisfied; save/load across waits and joins; cancel parallel listeners; verify reward delivery once; and verify cleanup without changing the committed outcome. Apply the block-specific cases in the [expanded contracts](../all-quest-blocks.md). No installed playtest is claimed.

## Connected paths and supporting bindings

1913 repeated typed/socket path signatures in this selected graph context; 330 distinct direct resource identities; 33 embedded graph handoffs. The full occurrence lists, concrete parameters, exact source pointers and external edges are retained in the checked evidence. Shared forward dependencies can serve several quests; these are context counts.

### Connected block candidates

These examples are ranked by breadth across resource families. Three nodes are a useful extraction seed, not a complete contract: retain all boundary ports and inspect surrounding setup, cancellation and cleanup.
